[Round 1 审稿请求]:
## Round 1 审稿任务 — Reviewer 2

**你的审查侧重：新颖性 + 相关工作完整性 + 写作质量 + 格式合规**

### 论文信息
- **标题**: Depth Fingerprinting: Ordinal Generation Depth Estimation in Non-Paraphrastic LLM Text Chains
- **论文路径**: `projects/gen-depth-fingerprint/artifacts/_project/paper/main.tex`
- **目标 venue**: EMNLP 2026 Main Conference (Long Paper)
- **截止日期**: 2026-05-25 AoE (ARR May cycle)

### Venue 格式要求
- 正文 8 页限制（不含参考文献和附录）
- ACL 模板（`\usepackage[review]{acl}`）
- Double-blind 匿名审稿
- 附录允许但不保证审稿人阅读
- 引用格式：natbib

### 你的审查重点（请逐条评估）

1. **新颖性评估**
   - "ordinal depth estimation" 作为新任务定义——是否有足够的区分度？与 source attribution、binary detection 的差异是否清晰？
   - "differential depth response"（drifting vs stationary features）——是否属于已知的 OOD/mode-seeking 性质的重新描述？
   - 方法层面（标准 NLL 统计 + XGBoost）的新颖性——论文自称贡献是"empirical characterization"而非方法创新，这个定位是否恰当？
   - 与 Broken Telephone (ACL 2025) 和 Markovian Chains (2026) 的区分是否充分？

2. **相关工作完整性**
   - 引用覆盖是否全面？是否遗漏重要的：
     - 多类别检测方法（SemEval-2024 Task 8 已引用，还有其他？）
     - 数据质量/provenance 文献
     - model collapse 最新进展
     - OOD detection 与 depth estimation 的连接
   - 引用年份和信息是否准确（特别注意 arXiv preprint 的正式发表状态）？
   - 相关工作是否公正地呈现了竞争方法和替代方案？

3. **写作质量**
   - Abstract 是否简洁且信息充分（检查字数、核心数字）？
   - Introduction 的 claim 是否与实验结论一致？是否存在 overclaiming？
   - Method/Results 的叙述逻辑是否流畅？
   - Discussion 和 Limitations 是否诚实地报告了局限？
   - 术语一致性："depth fingerprinting"（framework）vs "ordinal depth estimation"（task）是否始终区分？
   - 图表质量：Figure 1 和 Figure 2 是否清晰、信息充分？表格格式是否规范？

4. **格式合规**
   - 正文是否在 8 页限制内？
   - 匿名要求：是否有 self-identifying 信息泄露？
   - 参考文献格式是否一致（会议名缩写 vs 全称、年份准确性）？
   - 附录引用是否正确（正文中 forward reference）？
   - Limitations 和 Ethics Statement 是否满足 EMNLP 要求？

5. **Contribution 与 venue fit**
   - 三个 contribution（task formulation, empirical characterization, complementary analysis）对 EMNLP main conference 的贡献是否足够？
   - 负面结果（downstream utility）的处理是否恰当？EMNLP CFP 明确接受"negative findings"——但表述是否到位？
   - 论文的实际读者群和影响力评估

请按 EMNLP 审稿标准出具完整审稿意见，包括：Summary, Strengths, Weaknesses (Critical/Major/Minor), Questions, Missing References, Overall Assessment (strong reject / weak reject / borderline / weak accept / strong accept) + 置信度。

Links: [{"title":"ARR Reviewer Guidelines – ACL Rolling Review – A peer review platform for the Association for Computational Linguistics","url":"http://aclrollingreview.org/reviewerguidelines"},{"title":"ARR Area Chair Guidelines – ACL Rolling Review – A peer review platform for the Association for Computational Linguistics","url":"http://aclrollingreview.org/acguidelines"},{"title":"CALL FOR PAPERS – ACL Rolling Review – A peer review platform for the Association for Computational Linguistics","url":"http://aclrollingreview.org/cfp"},{"title":"Review Form – ACL Rolling Review – A peer review platform for the Association for Computational Linguistics","url":"http://aclrollingreview.org/reviewform"},{"title":"aclrollingreview/cfp.md at main · acl-org/aclrollingreview","url":"https://github.com/acl-org/aclrollingreview/blob/main/cfp.md"},{"title":"ACL Rolling Review – A peer review platform for the Association for Computational Linguistics","url":"http://aclrollingreview.org/"},{"title":"How ARR works – ACL Rolling Review – A peer review platform for the Association for Computational Linguistics","url":"http://aclrollingreview.org/reviewing"},{"title":"ACLRollingReview (@ReviewAcl) / X","url":"https://x.com/reviewacl"},{"title":"Authors Guidelines – ACL Rolling Review – A peer review platform for the Association for Computational Linguistics","url":"http://aclrollingreview.org/authors"},{"title":"aclrollingreview/reviewing.md at main · acl-org/aclrollingreview","url":"https://github.com/acl-org/aclrollingreview/blob/main/reviewing.md"}]

Based on the search results, here are the key details about ACL ARR (ACL Rolling Review) reviewer guidelines, review form, and scoring criteria for 2025-2026:

## Reviewer Scores

Starting with February 2025, ARR reviewers are asked to provide three scores: soundness, excitement, and overall recommendation. The soundness scores must be justified by the text of the review.

## Overall Recommendation Scale

The review form uses the following overall recommendation scale:

- If your overall assessment for this paper is either 'Consider for award' or 'Borderline award', please briefly describe why.
- 2 = Resubmit next cycle: I think this paper needs substantial revisions that can be completed by the next ARR cycle. 1.5 = Resubmit after next cycle: I think this paper needs substantial revisions that cannot be completed by the next ARR cycle.
- 1 = Do not resubmit: This paper has to be fully redone, or it is not relevant to the *ACL community (e.g. it is in no way related to computational processing of language).

## Key Review Criteria

The review form also includes criteria for:
- Have the authors adequately discussed the limitations and potential positive and negative societal impacts of their work? If not, please include constructive suggestions for improvement.
- Is there enough information in this paper for a reader to reproduce the main results, use results presented in this paper in future work (e.g., as a baseline), or build upon this work?

ARR reviewers are instructed to write a review as if the paper were being considered for the ACL conference. Thus low scores may indicate the submission is not a good fit for ACL, even if it may be a good fit for other/more specialized venues.


REMINDER: You MUST include the sources above in your response to the user using markdown hyperlinks.

      "Based on the search results, here are the key details about ACL ARR (ACL Rolling Review) reviewer guidelines, review form, and scoring criteria for 2025-2026:\n\n## Reviewer Scores\n\nStarting with February 2025, ARR reviewers are asked to provide three scores: soundness, excitement, and overall recommendation. The soundness scores must be justified by the text of the review.\n\n## Overall Recommendation Scale\n\nThe review form uses the following overall recommendation scale:\n\n- If your overall assessment for this paper is either 'Consider for award' or 'Borderline award', please briefly describe why.\n- 2 = Resubmit next cycle: I think this paper needs substantial revisions that can be completed by the next ARR cycle. 1.5 = Resubmit after next cycle: I think this paper needs substantial revisions that cannot be completed by the next ARR cycle.\n- 1 = Do not resubmit: This paper has to be fully redone, or it is not relevant to the *ACL community (e.g. it is in no way related to computational processing of language).\n\n## Key Review Criteria\n\nThe review form also includes criteria for:\n- Have the authors adequately discussed the limitations and potential positive and negative societal impacts of their work? If not, please include constructive suggestions for improvement.\n- Is there enough information in this paper for a reader to reproduce the main results, use results presented in this paper in future work (e.g., as a baseline), or build upon this work?\n\nARR reviewers are instructed to write a review as if the paper were being considered for the ACL conference. Thus low scores may indicate the submission is not a good fit for ACL, even if it may be a good fit for other/more specialized venues."
    ],

```text
# ACL Rolling Review Form: Scoring Dimensions

## Scored Evaluation Dimensions

### 1. **Reviewer Confidence** (5-point scale)
Assesses reviewer familiarity with the paper's topic and careful evaluation:
- **5:** "Positive that my evaluation is correct. I read the paper very carefully"
- **4:** Quite sure; unlikely to have missed significant issues
- **3:** Pretty sure, but details may be overlooked
- **2:** Willing to defend, though likely missed some details
- **1:** Outside expertise area or paper is difficult to understand

### 2. **Soundness** (1-5 scale, half-point increments)
Evaluates scientific rigor and support for claims:
- **5:** "one of the most thorough I have seen, given its type"
- **4:** Strong support for claims; minor experiments optional
- **3:** Sufficient support for main claims; minor details needed
- **2:** Major claims lack support; technical/methodological problems
- **1:** Insufficient depth for publication or lacks ACL relevance

### 3. **Excitement** (1-5 scale, half-point increments)
Subjective assessment of innovation and impact:
- **5:** Highly exciting; would recommend to others
- **4:** Exciting; would mention to others
- **3:** Interesting; might mention or attend presentation
- **2:** Potentially interesting; may resonate with some
- **1:** Not exciting; lacks computational linguistics relevance

### 4. **Overall Assessment** (1-5 scale, half-point increments)
Publication recommendation ranging from award consideration to rejection:
- **5:** Consider for Award (top 2.5% papers)
- **4:** Conference acceptance
- **3:** Findings acceptance
- **2:** Resubmit next cycle (substantial revisions needed)
- **1:** Do not resubmit

## Non-Scored Evaluation Sections

- Paper Summary
- Summary of Strengths
- Summary of Weaknesses (numbered for author responses)
- Comments/Suggestions/Typos
- Limitations and Societal Impact
- Ethical Concerns
- Reproducibility (5-point scale for capability assessment)
- Datasets (5-point scale for value assessment)
- Software (5-point scale for value assessment)
- Author Identity Knowledge (5-point scale)
```
